1b1(phal chen): @##//'phags pa sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba/bam po drug cu 
1b2(phal chen): rtsa gsum pa/de nas byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs chen po de'i nang nas/byang 
1b3(phal chen): chub sems dpa' kha cig 'di snyam du sems te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
1b4(phal chen): rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing na ji tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs 
2a1(phal chen): @##//rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang /'byung ba rnam par dag pa ni ji 
2a2(phal chen): tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdung ni 
2a3(phal chen): ji tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi bya ba/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas skye ba ni ji tsam zhig 
2a4(phal chen): tu bsam gyis mi khyab/sang sa rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ni ji tsam zhig tu 
2a5(phal chen): bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnamasagyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung ni ji tsam zhig tu 
2b1(phal chen): bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes ni ji tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi khyab/
2b2(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi de bzhin gshegasapa'i rnam par 'phrul pa ni ji tsam zhig tu bsams gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas
2b3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis chags pa mi mnga' ba'i rnam par spyod pa ni ji tsam zhig tu ba sam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgas bcom ldan 
2b4(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa ni ji tsam zhig tu ba'am gyis mi khyab snyam pa dang /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 
2b5(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi sems thugs kyis thugs chud nas/byang chub sems dpa' ut+pa la'i snying po/sangs rgyas 
2b6(phal chen): kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis byin tu brlabs so//sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi byin dang ldan par mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od 
3a1(phal chen): @##//kyis snang bar mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi gzi brjid kyis yongs su rgyas par mdzad do//sangs rgyas
3a2(phal chen): kyi mi 'jig pa la rab tu bzhag go/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa khong du chud par mdzad do//sangs rgya kyi bdag
3a3(phal chen): nyid che ba nyid dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi yul rab tu rtogs par mdzad do//chags pa med pa'i rgyud 'byam klas pa la rab tu bzhag go//
3a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas thams cad gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa'i rigs rab tu rtogs par mdzad//sangs rgyas kyi tshul gyi gnas brjod 
3a5(phal chen): du med pa la rab tu bzhag pa mdzad do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ut+pa la'i snying pos chos kyi dbyings la 'jug ba rig cing 
3a6(phal chen): khong du chud pas/chags pa med pa'i dbyings su bsgrubs pa'i yid dang /chos thams cad la bsgribs pa med par gnas 
3b1(phal chen): ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang ldan pa'i yang dag par 'jug pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su song ba'i sangs 
3b2(phal chen): rgyas blta ba dang /snying rje chen pos lta ba'i sems can thams cad yongs su sbyongs pa'i go cha chen po dang /byang chub sems dpa'i yi dam dang /spyod pa'i 
3b3(phal chen): dkyil 'khor rgyan chad pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes mngon par sgrub pa skad cig gi cha shas la rnam par spyod pa dang /thams cad mkhyen 
3b4(phal chen): pa nyid kyi sgo mthong ba mi zad pa dang /tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa'i rnam pa/gzungs thams cad kyis snang bar byas pa dang ldan pas sangs rgyas kyi 
3b5(phal chen): mthus byang chub sems dpa' padamo'i snying po la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni tshad med pa la rnam par spyod pa 
3b6(phal chen): ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/rnam par 'phrul pa chen po la rnam par spyod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras sang rgyas bcom 
3b7(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa thams cad la sku'i mtshan nyid thams cad rab tu brnyes pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi 
4a1(phal chen): @##//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba ni ting nge 'dzin dang dgongs pas sbyar ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tha dad pa yang 
4a2(phal chen): dag par mkhyen pa bzhi ni brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du mang ba shin tu ston pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams 
4a3(phal chen): cad la bsam gyis mi khyab par rnam par spyod pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi gsung dang dbyangs ni tshad med pa'o//sangs 
4a4(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi chos kyi dbyings thugs su chud pa ni brjod du med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi sangasargyas kyi khyu mchog gi 
4a5(phal chen): mthu phyung ba ni/thams cad mngon par mkhyen pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyis chos kyi dbyings thugs chud 
4a6(phal chen): cing bstan pa ni bsgribs pa med pa rim thar thug pa'o//kye rgyal ba'o sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasar rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings 
4a7(phal chen): mtha' dang dbung med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnam pa bcu ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
4b1(phal chen): rnyog pa med par rgyud thams cad du byung bas mtha' dang dbung med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chags pa'i spyan ni/chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi spyan 
4b2(phal chen): mngon par sangs rgyas pas mtha' dang dbung med ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi chags pa med pa'i snyan ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi sgra bsgribs pa med 
4b3(phal chen): par thugs su chud pas mtha' dang dbung medapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
4b4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi shangs kyi skye mched ni/sangs rgyas kyi mnga' thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 
4b5(phal chen): brnyes pas mtha' dang dbung mid pa'o//sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi ljags shin tu yangs pa ni/chos kyi dbyings thams cad du gsung gi dbyangs rjes su 'gro 
4b6(phal chen): bas mtha' dang dbung medaba'o//sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi sku'i chos nyid ni/sems can thams cad ma lus pa'i sems kyi rnam pa de bzhin gshegs 
4b7(phal chen): pa mthong ba rnam par dmigs pas mtha' dang dbung maid pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldang 'das rnams kyi thugs kyi phrin las ni/chos kyi sku dang tha mi dad cing /dus gsum 
4b8(phal chen): chags pa med par thugs su chud cing rnam par dag pas mtha' dang dbung me dpa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa bsgribs pa ma mnga' ba la 
5a1(phal chen): @#//nam mkhar spyod pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang dang /rnam par 'phrul pa dang /cho 'phrul mi zad pa yang dag par ston pas mtha' dang dpung med 
5a2(phal chen): pa'o||sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rnam par dag pa ni/brjod du med pa'i rgyan byin gyis brlabs pas/sangs 
5a3(phal chen): rgyas kyi zhing nye bar nyon mongs pa med pa yang dag par bstan te/bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dmigs par mdzad pas mtha' dang dpung me dpa'o//sangs 
5a4(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam pa kyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i khyad par dang /smon lam thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa ni/rnam par rol pa'i ye shes kyi chos 
5a5(phal chen): yongs su rdzogs te/chos thams cad rdzogs par byang chub par bya ba la mtha' dang dpung med pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 
5a6(phal chen): bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings mthar dang bcud med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po la 'jig na ni/
5a7(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi slad cig gcig la ye shes kyis mngon par bsgrubs pa yang dag par bstan pa'o//bcu gang zhe na/skad 
5b1(phal chen): cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du dka' ldan gyis gnas nas 'pho ba yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyis khams thams cad 
5b2(phal chen): du byang chub sems dpa' tshe yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rted gyi khams thams cad du mngon par 'byung ba yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig 
5b3(phal chen): gcig la 'jig rten gyi kham thams cad du byang chub kyi shing drung du bzhugs shing mngon par 'tshang brgya ba yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jig rtan gyi 
5b4(phal chen): khams thams cad du chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba yongs su bstan pa dangs/skad cig gcig la 'jigs rten gyi khams thams cad 'dul ba'i sems can yongs su smin 
5b5(phal chen): pa'i tshul ji lta ba bzhin du dus la bab par shes pas/sangs rgyas bsnyen bkur ba yongs su bstan pa dad/skad cig gcig la 'jigrtan gyi khams thams 
5b6(phal chen): cad du sems can ji ltar 'dul ba'i tshul bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa brjed du med pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi sku sna tshogs rnam par dmigs pa yongs su bstan 
5b7(phal chen): pa dang/skad cig gcig la'ajig rten gyi khams thams cad du rgyan sna tshogs dang/rgyan brjod du med pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i khyu mchog gi mthu 
6a1(phal chen): @#//byung ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i mdzod yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du rnam par dag pa gcig ma 
6a2(phal chen): yin pa thams cad yongs su bstan pa dang |skad cig gcig la 'jig te gya khams thams cad du 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas skye ba thams 
6a3(phal chen): cad du dbang po dang /brtson 'grus rnam pa tha dad byung ba dang /mos pa dang spyod pa la tha dad pa byung ba la dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi rgyud du mngon par 'tshang rgya ba yongs su bstan pa ste 
6a4(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi skad cig pa ye shes mngon par sgrub pa yang dag par bstan pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu 
6a5(phal chen): po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom? ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs? rgyas kyi dus las mi 'da' ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams mngon par 'tshang rgya ba ni de'i 
6a6(phal chen): dus las mi 'da' bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan? 'das rnams kyi dge ba rnam par smin pa dang /rnam par ma smin pa mnyam pa nyid ni de'i dus las mi 'da' 
6a7(phal chen): bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das? rnams kyis yongs su smin pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams lung bstan pa ni/de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus?
6b1(phal chen): bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sems can gyi bsam pa'i rgyud du cho 'phrul yongs su bstan pa ni de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs 
6b2(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyis sems can mos pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa'i tshul bzhin du sku yongs su bstan pa ni dus las mi 'da' bas dpal la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas
6b3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams btang snyomasu bzhugs pa ni/de'i dus las mi 'da' bar dus la bab pa yin/sadas rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams yul dang grong dang grong 
6b4(phal chen): rdal du gshegs pa ni/de'i dus la mi 'da' bas dus la babs pa yin/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams dad pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa 'i tshul bzhin du sems can yongasu
6b5(phal chen): 'dzin pa ni/de'i dus las mi da' bas dus la bab pa yino/sadas rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams sems can kun nas nyon mongs pa dang ldan zhing gdul dka' ba la
6b6(phal chen): gzhan la ltos pa mi mnga' bar 'dul ba ni de'i dus las mi 'anga' bas dus la bab pa yi no/sadas rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sadas rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa bsam gyis
6b7(phal chen): mi khyab pas rnam par 'phrul pa bstan pa de'i dus las mi 'da' ba na dus la bab pa yin gyi rgyal po sras bcu po de dag ni/sadas rgyas bcom ldan 
7a1(phal chen): @#//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi yul dang med cing bsam gyis mi khyab ste/bcu 
7a2(phal chen): gang zhe na/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ma lus par phyi la mo kyang gcig gis rgyas par dag pa dang /dus gcig tu sangs rgyas thams cad yongs su bstan 
7a3(phal chen): pa dang /'od zer gcig gis 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad snang bar bya ba dang /sku gzugs gcig gi lus dang /gzugs thams cad yongs su bsten 
7a4(phal chen): pa dang /gnas gcig na bzhugs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams thams chad ma lus par byen pa'i skur 'gyur ba dang /ye shes gcig rtogs pas chas thams cad 
7a5(phal chen): la bsgribs pa med par 'gyur ba dang /thugs gcig bskyed pas chos kyi dbyings thams cad ma lus par/rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi che ba nyid tshad 
7a6(phal chen): med pa dang ldan pa'i gshegs stabs gcig gis de kho na nyid yang dag par bstan pa dang /dus gsum thams cad sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang gis rgyas par 'gengs pa dang 
7a7(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad dang ma 'dres par bzhugs shing /'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis 
7b1(phal chen): rgyud kyi tshul gyi gcig pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas su bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi yul dang med cing bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o/kye rgyal 
7b2(phal chen): ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zin gyis brlabs mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes te/bcu gang zhe na 'bud med pa'i chos thams 
7b3(phal chen): cad la 'jug pas/smon lam mngon par sgrub pa'i ye she sa dang /lus med pa la lus mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shais dang /chos thams cad mi gnyis pa la mngon par 'tshang 
7b4(phal chen): rgya ba mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shis dang /sems can kyi mthar thug par bdag med pa la sems can 'dul ba mngon par sgrub pa'i yi shes dang /chos thams cad mtshan ma 
7b5(phal chen): med pa la/mtshan ma sna tshogs mngon par sgrub pa'i ye zhes dang /'jig rten gyi khams kyi yul ma skyes pa la 'jig rten gyi khams 'du ba dang /'jig pa mngon par 
7b6(phal chen): sgrub pa'i ye shes dang /yul thams cad mngon par 'du bya ba med pa la las kyi rnam par smin pa mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shese dang /chos thams cad brjod du med pa la chos 
7b7(phal chen): kyi dbyings su gsung mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes dang /chos thams can kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang ba 'jug pa med pa la kun nas nyon mongs pa dag/rnam par 
8a1(phal chen): @#//byang ba mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes dang /chos thams cad ma skyes pa la skye ba dang /'jig pa'i mtshan nyid rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba mngon par sgrub 
8a2(phal chen): pa'i ye shes te/rtsa rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mngon par sgrub pa'i ye she so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu 
8a3(phal chen): po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshad med pa'i khong su chud pa dag ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'des rnams kyi sku ni dus gsum thams 
8a4(phal chen): cad kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i khong du chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan dang rnams sems can thams cad 'dul bar mdzad pa ni/cho 'phrul chen po gsum gyi khong su chud 
8a5(phal chen): pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom 'das rnams tshig rab tu dbye ba thams cad la mkhas pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi gzugs thams cad kyi khong su chud pa yin/
8a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi chos kyi 'khor lo thams cad ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa bzhir khongs su chud pa yin/sangs 
8a7(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam pa thams cad yongs su mi gtong ba ni/thugs rje chen po'i khong su chud pa yin/sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams rtag tu 
8b1(phal chen): mnyam par bzhag pa ni/dus thams cad du sems can thams cad la rnam par gzigs pa'i khongs su chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi 
8b2(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa ba ni/sems can rnam par gdul ba thams cad khongs su chud pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi skad cig gcig tu ni 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas skye 
8b3(phal chen): dbyings thams cad khongs su chud ba yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldang 'das rnams kyi skad cig gcig du ni 'das pa dang /ma byin pa dang /da ltar byung pa'i sangs rgyas skye 
8b4(phal chen): ba thams cad khongs su chung ba yin//sangs rgyas bcom lan 'das rnams kyi nyi ma gcig tu ni/dus gsum gyi bskal pa'i grangs khongs su chud par bstan pa thams 
8b5(phal chen): cad khongs su chud pa yin /kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi tshad med pa'i khongs su chud pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi 
8b6(phal chen): dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom lan 'dis rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par bka' ba rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/bdud kyi dkrugs ma rab tu 'joms zhing rnam par 
8b7(phal chen): 'thor ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi shin tu rgya cheba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//mu stegs can gzhan dang /phas kyi rgal ba tshar bcad pa ni/
9a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//'dul ba'i sems can thams cad rab tu tshim par mdzad pa 
9a2(phal chen): ni|sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin tu rgya che pa'i chos bzod par dka' pa'o//sems can 'dul ba'i dbang gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams 
9a3(phal chen): cad du gshegs pa ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasi rnams kyi shin tu rgya che pa bzod par dka' ba'o/chos kyi dbyings thugs su chud pa'i ye shes ni/
9a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba chos bzod par dka' ba 'o//'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ma lus par skus khyab cing /sangs rgyas 
9a5(phal chen): kyi sku rnam par phye ba sna tshogs thams cad du ma gnyis pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'o chos bzod par dka' 
9a6(phal chen): ba'o//gsung gi yan lag thams cad la sems can mos par bya ba dang /thos par bya ba dang /gdul bar bya ba don yod par tha dad bya yang dag par mkhyen pa thogs pa 
9a7(phal chen): med pas bstan pa'i tshul du tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa bzhi 'byung ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi shin tu rgya che 
9a8(phal chen): ba'i chos bzod par dka'
9b1(phal chen): pa'o||sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi ba spu'i bu ga gcig nas/de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed na/
9b2(phal chen): bsam gyis mi khyab cing thogs pa med pa'i tshul du'ang jig rten gyi khams thams chad kyi rdul shin tu phra pa snyed kyi thabs kyis 'byung zhing /de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyis 
9b3(phal chen): sku'i ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas 'byung ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag 
9b4(phal chen): par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdul phra ba gcig la sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad kyi rdul shin tu phra bsnyed yongs su ston cing /sangs rgyas kyi rgyan dang /rang bzhin gyi 
9b5(phal chen): lhub lhub dang /chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba rgyun mi 'chad cing /sems can rnam par 'dul ba thogs pa med la 'jig rten gyi khams de dag tu gtogs pa rnams la'ang mi bde ba dang /
9b6(phal chen): gnod pa med kyang rdul shin tu phra bde yang yang nges par ma byas/'jig rten gyi khams de dag kyang chung bar ma byas par ji ltar gnas pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyang thugs su chud pa'i ye shes mnga' 
9b7(phal chen): ba ni|sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi shin tu rgya che pa'i chos bzod par dka' pa'o//mtshun pa med pa'i khams de bzhin gshegs pas mngon par sangs rgyas te/sangs rgyas 
10a1(phal chen): @#//kyi chos thams cad la sun pa med cing bsgribs pa mi mnga' la/sems can pha rol gyi bsgribs pa sna tshogs kyi gnas par sel tha/de la bcom ldan 'das da 
10a2(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang ldan pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos la bzod par dka' ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/
10a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das//shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba 
10a4(phal chen): rnam pa thams cad du/kha na ma tho ba med cing /shin tu dag pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi yang dag par bsgrubs pa bdag nyid che ba ni/sngon 
10a5(phal chen): gyi mtha' nas rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs 
10a6(phal chen): rgyas kyi rgyud du rigs dang ldan par 'byung ba'i bdag nyid che ba ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rnam par dag pas/rnam pa thams cad du kha nam tho ba med cing shin tu rnam 
10a7(phal chen): par dag pa'o//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams ni rnam par shes pa la mi gnas pa ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid cho ba ni/phyi ma'i mtha'i 
10b1(phal chen): mur thug par gnas pas/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba mang cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni dus gsum la mi gnas par rnam par 
10b2(phal chen): spyod cing chos thams cad la rtan pa yang dag par bcom pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cad du khan ma tho ba med cing shin tu 
10b3(phal chen): rnam par dag pa'o//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasani dngos po rnam pa tha dad pa la rnam par gzhag pa gcig gis len pa med pas/sangs rgyas bcom 
10b4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che bani/rnam pa thams cad du kho na ma tho ba med cing shinatu rnam par dag pa'o//
10b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnam pa thams cad kyi bdag nyid che ba ni rnam pa thams cad ni kha na ma tho ba med pa ni rnam par 
10b6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag/rnam pa thams cad ni de bzhin du rnam par dag pa'o//
11a1(phal chen): @#//kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mi 'jigs pa bzhi brnyes pas bag tsha ba mi mnga' bar bzhugs 
11a2(phal chen): shing khyu mchog dang seng ge'i nga ro bgyin te/rgal ba'i nang na stag lta bu'i mthu dang ldan la/chos thams cad bstan pa la thugs sgron ma lta bu mnga' bas/sangs 
11a3(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing /shin tu rnam par dag pa'o //sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni 
11a4(phal chen): yongs su mya ngan las 'das nas bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa zhig lon yang mtshan gyi tshig thos pa tsam gyis bzhugs pa dang 'dra bar sems 
11a5(phal chen): can rnams kyi yon shin tu sbyong zhing /mya ngan las 'das nas de srid kyi yun ring po 'das kyang /sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i yon zad par mi 'gyur bas/sangs 
11a6(phal chen): rgyas kyi bdag nyid che ba ni//rnam pa thams cad du kha nam tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam pa 'jig rten gyi khams 
11a7(phal chen): brjod du med pa'i yang brjong du med pa dag na bzhugs shing /'jig rten gyi khams rgyang ring po na bzhugs kyang /'jig rten gyi khams/'di na gnas pa'i sems can 
11b1(phal chen): rnams kyis rjes su dran par byas na/sku mngon par ston pa mdzad pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing 
11b2(phal chen): shin tu rnam par dag pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi bdag nyid che pa ni/rnam pa thams cad kha na ma tho ba med 
11b3(phal chen): cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'i de dag ni/sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi shin tu rnam par dag pa ste bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas 
11b4(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi tshangs pa'i spyod pa rnam par dag pa la/sngon gyi smon lam gyis shin tu rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
11b5(phal chen): 'das rnams ni chags pa med pa'i tshul khrims kyis shin tu rnam par dag ba yin/sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs 
11b6(phal chen): pa sel bas/shin tu rnam par dag pa yi sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gis shin tu rnam par dag yin/sangs rgyas 
11b7(phal chen): bcom dan 'das rnams na'i khor gyis shin tu rnam par dbang pa yin/sangs rgyas bco ma ldan 'das rnams ni rigs rnam par dag pa/shin tu rnam par dag
12a1(phal chen): @#//pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni gzugs kyi sku'i mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po rnam par dag pas/shin tu rnam par dag pa yin/
12a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni/chos kyi sku rnyog pa med pas/shin tu rnam par dang pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom 
12a3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi phung po bsgribs pa med pas shin tu rnam par dag pa 
12a4(phal chen): yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa thams cad la mthu chen pa brnyes shing /pha rol tu phyin pa dam pa brnyes pas shin tu rnam par 
12a5(phal chen): dag pa yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi mdzad pa 
12a6(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 'byung zhing dus thams cad du gnas pa ste/bcug she na/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i thob pa'i sems can rnams la dus thams 
12a7(phal chen): cad du/sangs rgyas bstan pa dang /sems can sems can dang ldan pa rnams la/dus thams cad du chos bstan pa dang /sems 
12a8(phal chen): can sangs rgyas la dad pa'i 
12b1(phal chen): sems dang ldan pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba dus thams cad du bskyed pa dang /chos kyi skyon med par 'das pa'i sems can rnams dus thams cad du ye shes khong du chud par bya ba dang /'gro ba thams 
12b2(phal chen): cad du sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa dus thams cad du rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad dus thams cad du chags pa mi mnga' bar gshegs 
12b3(phal chen): pa dang /sems can thams cad la thugs rje chen po dus thams cad du mi gtong ba dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa dus thams cad du thogs 
12b4(phal chen): pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom lnga ni 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyasakyi rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs dus thams cad du thogs pa med pa dang /sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams
12b5(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings gnas pa'i sangs rgyas dus thams cad du 'byung ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
12b6(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi mdzad pa/'jig rten gyi 
12b7(phal chen): khams thams cad du 'byung zhing /dus thams cad du gnas pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu pa 'di dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs
12b8(phal chen): rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i tshul rnams ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
13a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las tshad mang ba brnyes pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
13a2(phal chen): 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang /legs pa mang po mnga' bas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni dge ba'i rtsa ba chad mang ba/bsam gyis mi khyab pa mang ba 
13a3(phal chen): mkhyen pa'i spyan lam dang khyab par mnga' bas ye shes rgya mcho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can gyi rigs thams cad ma lus pa'i sangs 
13a4(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad par bsgrub pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi bzad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcos ldan 'das rnams ni semasacan gyi rigs thams cad 
13a5(phal chen): ma lus pa'i sangs rgyas dang spyod pa la ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad dang //sangs rgyas bcom ldana'i 'das rnams ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i bsod nams zil gyis mi non pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi 
13b1(phal chen): zad pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po dang ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sris 
13b2(phal chen): bcu pa 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyis mngon par 
13b3(phal chen): byung ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste/chos thams cad la mi mkhyen pa gang yang mi mnga' ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom 
13b4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste/thams cad kyang thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom/ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yid no//'di lta ste/thams cad 
13b5(phal chen): kyang stobs bcu tshad med pa dang ldan pa 'di ni/sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi tshul yin no//'di 
13b6(phal chen): lta ste/thams cad kyang bla na med pa'i chos kyi 'khor la rab tu 
13b7(phal chen): bster ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi tshul yin no//'di lta 
13b8(phal chen): ste/thams cad kyang sems can 'dul ba yang dag par zhugs pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas 
13b9(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul 'di ni/'di lta ste/thams cad kyi don du mngon par sangs rgyas pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas pa 
14a1(phal chen): @#//kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste/'dul ba'i sems can rnams 'dul ba la thogs pa mi mnga' ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul 
14a2(phal chen): yin no//'di lta ste/mi gnyis par yang dag par spyod pa'i mtshan nyid kyi chos la rab tu gzhol ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas pa com ldan 'das rnams kyi tshu la yin no//'di 
14a3(phal chen): lta ste ji ltar yongs su smin par gyur pa dang /dul bar gyur pa'i sems can rnams la/sangs rgyas kyi yul cu med pas/sangs rgyas kyi yongs su mya ngan las 'da' 
14a4(phal chen): ba ston pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcol ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi 
14a5(phal chen): tshul lo//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'i 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcol rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos mtha' yas pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgo rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas 
14a6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can gyi khams shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni soms can gyi spyod pa sna tshogs shin 
14a7(phal chen): tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' ya sa pa'o//sangs rgyas bcol ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can thams cad kyi las dang /rnam par smin pa shin tu bstan pa'i skor mtha' yas 
14b1(phal chen): pa'o||sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can thams cad la gdams ngag dang /man ngag shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas 
14b2(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sims can mtha' yas pa rnam par sbyong ba'i nan tan gyi sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sadas rgyas bcom ldan 'das rna mas ni byang chub sems 
14b3(phal chen): dpa'i spyod pa thams cad la gdams ngagadang /man ngag gis yongs su gzhog pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni byang chub sems 
14b4(phal chen): pa'i khyod par gyi smon lam rnam pa tha dad pas 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa'i sgor ma tha' yas pa'o//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 
14b5(phal chen): ma lus pa'i 'du ba dang /'jig pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mjal yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasar rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa ni byang chub 
14b6(phal chen): sems dpa'i bsam pa dang /mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs su bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yasapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams 
14b7(phal chen): ma lus pa thams cad du 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa ni bskal pa yangs pa de dang de dag tu bstan cing 
15a1(phal chen): @#//brjod pa dang /yongs su bshad pas/sangs rgyas kyi ye shes thams cad khong du chud par bya ba'i sgor mtha' yas pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de 
15a2(phal chen): dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos mtha' yas pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgo'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'i 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas 
15a3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sems can rnams la sgrub pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
15a4(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sku yongs su bstan pa ni/sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub pa//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi gsung yang sems can rnams la sangs/rgyas kyi 
15a5(phal chen): phrin las sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams len pa mdzad pa yang sems can rnams las sangs rgyas kyi phrin la sgrub po//sangs rgyas 
15a6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'da'as rnams len pa mi mdzad pa yang sems canarnams la sangs rgyas kyi phren las sgrub po//sa dang /chu dang /me dang /rlung gis kyang /sangs 
15a7(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam sems can rnams la sangs sangs rgyas kyi phrin las/sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom/'das rnams ni byin gyi rlabs kyi yul dang na bas 
15b1(phal chen): dmigs pa thams cad yul du mdzad pas kyang /sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mtshan kyang sems 
15b2(phal chen): can rnams la sangs rgyas gyi phrin las sgrub po//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni/zhing la dmigs par mdzad pas kyang sems can rnams la sangs rgus 
15b3(phal chen): kyi phrin las sgrub po//sad rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamams ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pas kyang /sems can rna mas la sangs rgyas kyi phrani las sgrub 
15b4(phal chen): po||sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams mi gsung bar bzhugs pa yang sems can rnams la sad rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu 
15b5(phal chen): po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin lasasems can rnams la sgrub pa'o//bam po drug cu rtsa bzhi pa//kye 
15b6(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos te/bcu gang zhe na/smon lam chen po la rab tu 
15b7(phal chen): brtan zhing smon lam zhig ba ma mnga' bas ji skad du gsung ba de ltar mdzad cing gnyisu mi gsung ba 'di ni/sems can gyi gtso bo'i cho sad pa'o//phyi ma'i mtha'i mur dug 
16a1(phal chen): @#//ba'i skal pa thams cad du/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la yon tan thams cad yongs su rdzogs par mdzad par rab tu brtson zhing skyid lug pa mi mnga' ba 
16a2(phal chen): 'di ni/sems can gyi gtso bo 'tshos gnyis pa'o//sems can 'dul bar 'gyur ba gcig cig gzigs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med par 
16a3(phal chen): gshegs shing 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du gshegs pa la thags thogs med pa'i ni/sems can gyi gtso po'i chos gsum pa'o//zhe sdang ba'i sems can rnams 
16a4(phal chen): la thugs rje chen pos snyoms pa dang /dad pa'i sems can rnams la thugs rje chen pos snyom pa 'di ni/sems can gyi gtsob bo'i chos bzhi pa'o//gang gi tshe bla na med pa 
16a5(phal chen): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par bzhugs nas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byad chub tu mngon par sangs rgyas kyi bar du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 
16a6(phal chen): bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas su 'gyur ro snyam du/byang chub kyi sems ma nyams pa 'di ni/sems can kyi gtso bo'i chos lnga pa'o//dge ba'i rtsa ba 
16a7(phal chen): thams cad yang dag par sgrub cing thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du rong su rdo bas 'jug gi/'jig rten pa'i rgyud la ma yin pa 'di ni/sems can gyis gtso bo la chos drug pa'o//
16b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su sngon/sangs rgyas kyis spyad pa'i sems su lus kyi las dang /ngag gi las dang /yid kyi las kyis slob cing nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas 
16b2(phal chen): kyi spyod pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su sngo ba med pas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par sangs rgyas pa 'di ni/sems can gyi gtso 
16b3(phal chen): bo'i chos bdun pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i sems rnam par dag cing /'od gsal la dangs pa/thams cad du snang zhing kun tu 'od 'byung ba/sangs rgyas kyi chos 
16b4(phal chen): thams cad la snang zhing thams cad du snyoms pas thams cad mkhyen pa nid yongs su rdzogs par byed pa 'di ni/sems can gi gtso bo'i chos brgyad pa'o//'jig rten pa'i 
16b5(phal chen): bde ba thams cad yongs su spangs te/'jig rten pa'i dga' ba thams cad la ma chags pa dang /'jig rten thams cad kyis gos pa med pa'i smon lam thams cad du mnyam pa 
16b6(phal chen): nyid kyis 'jig rten thams cad bde ba thams cad dang ldan par byas te/sdug bsngal dang /spros pa thams cad rab tu 'jom pa 'di ni/sems can gyis gtso bo'i chos dgu pa'o//sdug 
16b7(phal chen): bsngal gyi mi bde ba thams cad nyams su bab ste/'gro ba thams cad kyi don du dka' thub kyis shin tu gdungs kyang bla bar sems bskyed pas/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 
17a1(phal chen): @#//'phags yongs su da srung ba dang ldan pa dang /stobs bcu'i sa'i bar du/sangs rgyas kyi yul la mos pas mthar thug pa 'di ni/sems can gyi gtso 
17a2(phal chen): bo'i chos bcu pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sems can gyi gtso bo 'chos so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di 
17a3(phal chen): dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chags pa med pa rnams ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
17a4(phal chen): 'das rnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du gshegs pas chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni/'jig 
17a5(phal chen): rten gyi khams ma lus pa thabs cad na bzhugs pas chags pa mi mnga'abar rnam par spyod pa yin no/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
17b1(phal chen): pa thams cad kyi dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs kyang /chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus 
17b2(phal chen): pa thams cad kyi sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha' dang /da ltar byung ba'i mtha' la chags pa mi mnga' bar rnams par spyod pa yin/sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnams
17b3(phal chen): ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad tsam gyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor na bzhugs kyang chags pa mi mnga' bar ranam par spyod pa yin/sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das
17b4(phal chen): rnams ni sems can ma lus pa thams cad kyi sems dang spyod pa la gzigs shing thugs kyi skad cig gcig la/cho 'phrul chen po gsum gyi spyi/sems can 
17b5(phal chen): rnam par 'dul yang chags pa mi mnga' bar nam par spyod pa yin/sar ma rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni sku gcig gis kyang tshad med pas ma gyis mi khyab pa'i 
17b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chen por rnam par spyod kyang chags pa mi mnga' par rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas btsam ldan 'das rnams ni sems can gyi khams

18a1(phal chen): @#//chang 'bar rnam par sbyong ba yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par snod pa chags pa

33b3(phal chen): pa dang /sems can rnam gzhan thob med pa'i sgor sgrub pa dang /'jig rten thams cad du 'byung ba'i sgo rab tu bstan pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rgyal mtshan yang dag par

84b3(phal chen): stong phrag bcu dang /spos kyi sprin stong phrag bcu dang /rol mo'i sprin stong phrag bcu dang rgyal mtshan gyi sprin stong phrag bcu dang bdugs kyi sprin stong phrag

84b6(phal chen): po dag byang chub sems dpa' de ni lha yul 'di nas 'phos te/mi'i 'jig rten du rgyal po zas gtsang ma'i khyim du lha mo sgyu ma'i sku na tsan dan gyi khang pa brtsags

85a1(phal chen): @#//'dzin dri ma dang bral ba la mnyam par bzhag pa ni mig gis snang ba ma yin yang /skye ba'i skye mched thams cad du snang ste/'on kyang byang chub sems

85a3(phal chen): rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems spyad la bsam pa yongs su sbyong ste/spyod pa la mnos la lam gyi sgrib pa dang /nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa dang /rnam par smin pa'i sgrib 
85a4(phal chen): pa dang /lta ba'i sgrib pa bshags pa gyis te/lus kyi las dang /ngag gi las dang /yid kyi las kun tu bzang po chos kyi dbyings kyis byas pa sems can gyi khams

85a7(phal chen): par shog shig/de skad ces brjod pa dang /lha'i bu de dag ngo mtshar dang rmad du gyur te/rab tu dga' zhing sems bde bar gyur nas/yang lha'i rnga de la byang chub sems dang 
85b1(phal chen): rnams sdig pa 'chags pa'i rnam pa ji lta bu zhig yod ces phyir 'dri'o//de nas lha'i rnga dbyang chub sems dpa' de'i ting nge 'dzin gyi byin rlabs dang /lha'i bu de dag gi dge ba'i rtsa 
85b2(phal chen): ba'i stobs dang /byin gyi brlabs kyis rnam pa 'di lta bu'i sgra 'byin to//lha'i sras pa dag/las ni shar phyogs nas 'ongs te/sems la gnas pa ma yin/'dug 
85b3(phal chen): dam yin/tshogs su bsags pa ma yin/phung por gyur ba ma yin te/lho phyogs nas ma yin/nub phyogs nas ma yin/byang phyogs nas ma yin/las 
85b4(phal chen): de ni phyogs bcu kun nas kyang 'ongs te gnas pa ma yin no//de ltar don byang chub sems dpas phyin ci log las byung ba ste/gnas med par yang dag par shes pas de'i 
85b5(phal chen): phyir de la 'gyod pa med do//lha'i sras po dag 'di lta ste dper na/lha'i rnga bdag ni las kyi rnam par smin pa ji lta ba dang /spyod pa ji lta ba dang /tshul khrims ji lta ba dang /

86a1(phal chen): @#//dang /gti mug dang /las dang /bya ba'i gsung yang rjod de/lha'i sras po dag bdag ces bya ba med do//bdag med par bdag gi zhes bya ba mi dmigs so//
86a2(phal chen): de dag dkri pas kyi rnam pa lam gyi rgyud drug du rnam par smin par 'gyur bar byas na de dag kyang shar phyogs nas mi dmigs so//lho phyogs nas ma yin/nub phyogs 
86a3(phal chen): nas ma yin/byang phyogs nas ma yin te/phyogs bcu kun nas kyang dmigs par mi rung ngo//lha'i sras po dag 'di lta ste/dper na/bdag gi sgra na lha'i

99a6(phal chen): pa'i 'jig rten sgron ma'i dam par 'gyur bya ste//stobs bcu mnga' ba kun mkhyen par//mi'i seng ger bya zhes smon lam 'debs//nged kyis sems can thams cad kyi//
99a7(phal chen): 'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rab tu 'bar gyur pa'i//sdug bsngal rnams las rab dkrol te//ngan song thams cad yongs su bcad bya zhes//'di 'dra'i smon lam rdzogs 
99b1(phal chen): byas te//de nas dpa' ba chen po spyod pa rab la spyod//blo brtan phyir ldog mi 'gyur ba//stobs bcu rnams kyi chags med rab tu 'thob//skyes bu seng ge gang rnams kyis//
99b2(phal chen): 'di 'dri'i smon lam sngar btab phyir ldog mi 'gyur bar//'jig rten rnams na spyod mdzad pa//de dag don yod spyod pa brjod par bya//bskal pa bzang po gcig la yang 
99b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas stong 'grangs tshang bar kun kyang 'byung 'gyur te//kun tu gzigs pa gang brjod pa//de dag rim gyis bshad pa 'di 'dra'o//bskal pa bzang po gci bzhin du//
99b4(phal chen): brjod du tshad pa'i bskal pa khrag khrig de bzhin te//sangs rgyas ma byon de dag gi/bzang po'i spyod pa bdag gis bstan par bya//zhing rgyud gcig po ji bzhin du//brjod du 
99b5(phal chen): med pa'i zhing rnams thams cad kun bzung ste//ma byon stobs bcu'i skyon med pa'i//spyod pa thams cad de bzhin bshad par bya//rgyal ba gang 'og gang 'byung dang //
99b6(phal chen): ci mtshan de'i smon lam rnam pa ci 'dra dang //rgyal ba gang gis gang gi lung //bstan mdzad mkhas pa'i skye tshe ji tsam dang //chos kyi rnam pa dge ba ni//gang gis 
99b7(phal chen): bsgrubs te dam pa brnyes par gyur pa dang //'jig rten ji ltar btul ba dang //rgyal ba rnams kyi dam chos cir gnas dang //rnam dag zhing yang ci 'dra dang //sems 
100a1(phal chen): @#//can rnam par dag dang chos kyi 'khor lo dang //bstan pa'i dus dang dus min dang //de las gang zag rnam par dag 'gyur dang //sems can 
100a2(phal chen): rnams kyi las gang dang //spyod pa gang dang rnam grol ci 'drar 'gyur ba dang //dbang po rno rtul bar ma dang //spyod mchog sems can 'dul ba'ang bstan par bya//
100a3(phal chen): 'di 'dra'i shes par 'jug pa la//spyod pa'i dam pa rnams kyis rab tu zhugs gyur cing //bzang po'i 'byung gnas spyod pa dang //'gro kun ye shes 'khor lo spyod 
100a4(phal chen): zhugs pa//lus kyi las la chags med cing //ngag la chags med sgra dbyangs thams cad rnam dag la//sems spyod chags pa med pa yis//dus 
100a5(phal chen): gsum ma lus rgyas par khyab par byed//'di 'dra kun tu bzang po yi//spyod pa'i mchog la pha rol phyin pa thob gyur pa//brjod du med pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams 
100a6(phal chen): chos dbyings kun tu rab tu brjod pa byed//ma 'ongs dus kyi bskal pa dang //'byam rdzas zhing rnams brjod du med pa thams cad kyang //skad cig sems kyis 
100a7(phal chen): rab tu shes//chos nyid de la rnam par rtog pa med//gang rnams spyod pa'i sa 'di la//spyod pa'i dam pas rjes su zhugs par gyur pa'i rnam par/byang chub sems 
100b1(phal chen): dpa' mchog rnams kyi//yon tan spyod pa phyogs tsam gshad par bya//mtha' dang dbus med ye shes can//stobs bcu rnams kyi spyod yul mchog la zhugs gyur 
100b2(phal chen): pa||thams cad shes byed mkhas pa'i rnams//mi ldog spyod pas rab tu zhugs gyur cing //'od byed blo gros dam pa dang //kun tu bzang po'i smon lam mchog 
100b3(phal chen): la zhugs gyur pa//dpe zla med pa de dag gi/ye shes tshul dang spyod pa brjod par bya//rdul phran gcig gi cha shes la//'jig rten khams rnams ji snyed de dag 
100b4(phal chen): shes pa dang //spyod pa rgya che thos gyur na//'jig rten de dag thams cad mos par 'gyur//rdul phran gcig la ji lta ba//de bzhin rdul phran thams cad ma lus 
100b5(phal chen): gzung bya ste//ye shes bzang po de dag ni//'jig rten khams la 'jug pa bsam mi khyab//de dag dus kyi rnam pa la'ang //rdul phran gcig gi khyon gyi cha la yang dag 
100b6(phal chen): 'jug/rdul phran gcig la de bzhin du//nying yangs rnam pa tha dad kun la 'jug/rdul phran thams cad re re la//zhing rnams brjod du med pa mang po 'dzud pa byed//
100b7(phal chen): rnam pa tha dad du ma dang //gcig dang tshad med de bzhin rab tu shes//ji snyed ci yang ma lus pa'i//chos dbyings gzung ba thams cad yang dag gzung byed cing //
101a1(phal chen): @#//mtshan nyid tha dad sna tshogs te//sems can grangs kyi bskal pa rgyud mi mnyam//'jig rten phra bar 'jug pa la//phra la shin tu gzhol bas 
101a2(phal chen): phra bar 'jug bya ste//brjod du med pa rab bstan pa'i//bskal pa thams cad ma lus mdzub pa byed//de dag ring dang thung ba dang //dus kyi skad cig sems can 
101a3(phal chen): skal ba mi mthun shes//shes spyod mi mnyam ma yin pa'i//shes pas de dag thams cad khong du chud//yangs dang yangs min mkhas pas na//'jig rten zab 
101a4(phal chen): mo rnams la rab tu 'jug pa byed//lus la zhing rnams de shes te//zhing la'ang de bzhin gcig min lus mdzud do//phyogs rnams bcu po thams cad du//phyogs 
101a5(phal chen): rnams kun la 'jug cing skad cig du ma dang //'jig rten khams rnams 'byam klas la//tshad med gzi brjid ldan pa rab tu 'jug/gang yang zhing rnams mtha' yas shing /
101a6(phal chen): dbus med gzung ba byas shing dus kyi dbyings su 'dzud//zhing gi rgyud la mkhas pa'i rnams//shes dang ldan pas ye shes rab tu 'byung //bzang po'i yon tan ldan pa'i 
101a7(phal chen): rnams//phyogs dang 'jig rten khams kyi rgyud du gyur pa dag/ji snyed yod pa der 'jug ste//brjod du med pa de dag rab tu shes//zhing dang zhing gis rnams kyi//brjod du 
101b1(phal chen): med pa'i zhing rnams kun kyang rab tu 'thob//de dag tshul yang rab tu shes//las rnams gang gis sbyangs pa'ang khong du chud//gang yang zhing rnams du ma dag/las rnams 
101b2(phal chen): khrag khrig kun gyis kun nas nyon mongs par//'gro bas kun tu bsgrub byas pa//de dag thams cad rgyud kyang ci 'dra shes//brjod du med pa yangs pa'i rnams//zhing gcig 
101b3(phal chen): bstan pas de dag yong su shes par gyur//de 'dri' zhing la 'jug pa dang //gang la grangs med de dag kun la 'jug//'jig rten khams ni gcig la yang //'jig 
101b4(phal chen): rten khams rnams thams cad yang dag 'dzud pa byed//'jig rten gcig tu bsgongs mi 'gyur//'jig rten khams rnams de bzhin 'dis mi 'gyur//spyi'u tshugs rnams dang 
101b5(phal chen): gong ma'i rnams//bla dang 'og gis rnams yongs su gzung ba ste//'gro ba'i rnam par ming thogs pa//'jig rten kun la 'jug cing rab tu shes//smre la zhing rgyu ba'i rgyud 
101b6(phal chen): rnams dang /zhing rnams mtha' yas mtha' dang dbus yod ma yin pa//sna tshogs yangs par bsham shes//rnam pa gcig shes rnam pa tha dad shes//zhing gi grangs 
101b7(phal chen): rnams rab tu shes//ye shes chags med mtha' ldan kun gyi grangs rnams kyang //bzang po'i theg pa spyod pa yis//rgyal sras kun tu bzang rnams rab tu shes//'jig 
102a1(phal chen): @#//rten thams cad sprul pa dang //sems can rnam par sprul dang de bzhin zhing sprul dang //chos rnams sprul pa'i tshul shes pa//sangs rgyas sprul pa'i pha rol 
102a2(phal chen): phyin pa'i rnams//'jig rten bra zhing zab pa dang //yangs shing sna tshogs yangs pa mtsho ma che dgu'i rnams//sna tshogs tha dad rnam pa sogs pa//las kyi rnam smin 
102a3(phal chen): gnas pa'i 'jig rten rnams//rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul pa yis//chos dbyings 'jug par bya la shin tu bsgrubs pa'i//rgyal sras mtha' dang dbus med pa//phyogs bcu 
102a4(phal chen): thams cad ma lus 'gengs par byed//'jig rten khams rnams de dag gi/ming ni bskal pa sems can snyed kyis mthar thug cing //zad par rjod par mi nus ste//ming bshad 
102a5(phal chen): gal te sangs rgyas gsung na gsung //'jig rten mgon po rnams kyi yang //mtshan rnams mtshan nyid mi mthun rnam pa sna tshogs mtshan//bskal pa brjod med zad byas kyang //
102a6(phal chen): mtshan rnams gcig nas gcig brgyud mthar thug med//'on kyang dmar ba'i sems med par//mkhas pa rnams ni dus gsum bde gshegs sangs rgyas phyir//chos kyi dbyings 
102a7(phal chen): la byung ba ste//sangs rgyas ye shes sa la'ang rab tu zhugs//dran pas chags med dag pa ste//chags med yid dang ldan zhing blo gros mtha' yas pas//ye shes blo gros mtshams 
102b1(phal chen): ldan pa//chos dbyings bshad la pha rol phyin pa thob//gang gis 'jig rten khams kyi rnams//yangs dang phra ba rnam pa sna tshogs shes gyur cing //der ni 'das pa'i dus rnams la//
102b2(phal chen): sems gcig bskyed pas rab tu 'jug pa byed//mi yi seng ge de dag tu//sangs rgyas spyod pa sna tshogs rnam pas chags med par//byang chub mchog tu sangs rgyas shing //rnam par 
102b3(phal chen): 'phrul pa thams cad ston pa mdzad//de bzhin ma 'ongs dus rnams kyi//rjes su gang byon de'i bskal pa ci 'dra dang //de la mi yi seng ge gang //skye bar 'gyur ba de dag kun kyang shes//smon 
102b4(phal chen): lam spyod pa ci 'dra dang //spyod yul gang rnams bsgom pa gang gis grub gyur cing //bsgrub pa'i rnam pa ci 'dra bas//bskal ba gang byang chub 'tshang rgya dang //de dag 'khor kyang khong du chud//
102b5(phal chen): sku tshe'i tshad dang sems can 'dul ba ci 'dra dang //chos kyi sgo mo gang zhig gis//'gro bar chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba shes//de ltar kun du bzang po yi//ye shes mchog gis bsgrubs 
102b6(phal chen): po'i spyod pa'i sa gnas shing //de dag thams cad rab tu shes//de ni 'gro gtso kun gyi 'byung gnas yin//de ltar byung pa gzung byas shing //'jig rten khams rnams thams cad 
102b7(phal chen): 'dab chags ma lus par//'jig rten khams la rab tu 'jug/de bzhin chos kyis dbyings rnams rab tu shes//des ni mi yi gtso bo rnams//dgra las rgyal zhing thams cad gzigs 
103a1(phal chen): @#//pa ji snyed pa//mi thub rgol ba dpa' bo'i rnams//chos kyi mnga' la pha rol phyin brnyes pa//de dag 'khor kyang rab tu shes//sems can 'dul dang zhing rnams 
103a2(phal chen): rnam par dag pa dang //rgyal ba'i rnam par 'phrul pa'i rnams//sems pas bskal pa yangs pa zad par byed//skyes bu'i mchog rnams 'dul mdzad cing //'jig rten 
103a3(phal chen): mchog tu phyin pa'i byin rlabs dam pa la//mi zad mdzad kyi shes yangs pas//ye shes tshul la mkhas pa rab tu 'jug/chags med mig gis blta ba dang //chags 
103a4(phal chen): med rna ba mngon par bsgrub pa sgrub byed cing //chags pa med pa'i lus yangs dang //yangs pa'i lces kyang 'jig rten tshim par byed//dam pa'i sems la chags med de//
103a5(phal chen): bsam pa kun tu rnam yangs rnam par dag dang ldan//ye shes 'byung gnas bdag nyid che//dus gsum sems rnams ma lus des khyab po//sprul pa'i pha rol phyin thob cing //
103a6(phal chen): zhing rnams sprul pa'i bslab par bya ba bslab pa'i rnams//sems can sprul la mkhas pa dag//sprul pa sprul pas 'jig rten 'dul ba byed//'du shes rnam pa du ma ste//des 
103a7(phal chen): ni 'jig rten sna tshogs ma lus rnam par bris//de dag thams cad rab tu shes//sangs rgyas ye shes tshul la rab tu 'jug/'khor rnams brjod du med pa dag/sangs rgyas 
103b1(phal chen): sku rnams re re dag la yod pa'i rnams//kun la 'jug cing mthong bar byed//sems can rnam par 'dul ba'i mtha' yang med//ye shes zab mo mthong bas na//ye shes nyi ma shin 
103b2(phal chen): tu gsal ba shar gyur pa'i//phyir mi ldog pa'i spyod pa la//'jig rten 'byam klas kun tu 'jug par byed//ji lta'i tshul du zhugs gyur cing //'jig rten 'di dag thams cad rnam par 
103b3(phal chen): bkod 'dra bar//sems can tshul bzhin rab tu 'phags//khams gsum thams cad mig yor tshul 'drang shes//'on kyang rtog dang rnam rtog med//'jig rten mtha' dang 
103b4(phal chen): dbur med kun la rtog mi byed//rtog dang rtog med mkhas pa ste//rtog pa'i shes pas rnam par rtog pa med//bskal pa brjod du med pa'i rnams//sems bskyed gcig la yang dag 
103b5(phal chen): 'du bar mdzad byed cing //'jig rten mi khom 'di mthong nas//skad cig sems kyis thams cad shes par byed//brjod du med pa'i zhing rnams kyang //sems bskyed gcig gis thams cad 
103b6(phal chen): yongs su 'da' bar byed//bskal pa brjod med zab byed kyang //skra'i rtse'i cha shas gcig las g.yo ba med//bskal pa brjod dus med pa dag/nyi ma gcig dang yud tsam gcig tu 
103b7(phal chen): 'dzud byed kyang //ring dang thung la gzhol ba med//skad cig gcig la 'jug pa'i pha rol phyin//sems kyi rnam pa 'jig rten kun//'jig rten rnam pa sems kun gyi sems yin te//
104a1(phal chen): @#//dam pa gnyis kyi grangs su zhugs//'on kyang dpa' mi gnyis rtog pa med//sgyu ma'i rnam pa 'jig rten te//sems can zhing dang sangs rgyas chos kyang de 
104a2(phal chen): bzhin no//'on kyang 'jig rten mgon po'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun la gcig tu sto//mas//lus rnams brjod du med pa yis//phyogs bcu thams cad rgyas par 
104a3(phal chen): bkang zhing yongs su ston//'on kyang lus la gzhol ba med//chos nyid rten pa gnas med rab tu shes//'on kyang mi yi seng ge rnams//gnyis la gnas pa'i shes pas byung ba ma 
104a4(phal chen): yin te//mi gnyis pa la'ang gnas pa med//gnyis dang gnyis med chos rnams rab tu shes//ji ltar smig rgyu rab rib dang //mig yong smra bar na de bzhin rmi lam 'dra ba dang /

104a7(phal chen): sgom//sngon nas nyon mongs nyams med pa'i//bsam pa dag pas 'jig rten kun tu 'gro//mi zad mkha' ltar rnam dag pa'i//rgya che shin tu yangs pa'i sa la rab tu zhugs 
104b1(phal chen): gsung ba'i seng ge thams cad kyi//tshul gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad rjes su dran//'jig rten rab tu spros pa la//smon lam tshad med yongs su gdab pa byas pas na//de dag

104b3(phal chen): 'jig rten rnams dang 'jig rten chos//chags med tha dad tshang ba'i tshul du shes//chos kyi sku'i snying po can//de'i lus la 'jig rten thams cad gzung byas shing //'jig rten

104b5(phal chen): 'jig rten thams cad kyi//rjes su lus 'gro der song 'phos pa med //de ltar 'jig rten thams cad kyis//gos pa med cing 'jig rten yongs su dag pas na//lus ni mkha' 
104b6(phal chen): dang 'dra ba ste//de'i las rnams gang yang skyes pa med//mi zad pa yi lus shes pas//de ni nam yang skyes shes dgags pa med pa ste//rtag dang mi rtag ma yin pas 
104b7(phal chen): 'jig rten kun la'ang rnam par dmigs pa byed//lta ba'i rnam par gtugs pa//brtan pa de dag yang dag blta ba ston pa byed//bdag gi med cing gnas pa med//
105a1(phal chen): @#//len pa med cing chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam//ji ltar sgyu ma 'jam pas ba zhig/sna tshogs mtshan nyid rnam pa tha dad gzugs 'phrul byed//'on kyang de la 
105a2(phal chen): 'gro ba med//gang du 'gro ba de bzhin mi dmigs so//sgyu ma tshad mar ma yin te//sgyu ma tshad kyi mi dmigs par bya ba'ang ma yin no//tshad med tshad dang ldan pa yang //tshogs 
105a3(phal chen): kyi dbus su ston pa rab tu byed//de bzhin rtogs pa'i blo ldan pa//sangs rgyas kun gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams las byung //sems ni tshad ma yin te//tshad med pa la

105b1(phal chen): gyur kyang //'jig rten thams cad ma lus de dag khong du chud//'jig rten thams cad mi lus par//rnam par sangs rgyas mya ngan gar mi 'da'//chos rnams bzang ba'i tshul la

106a1(phal chen): @#//med shin tu yangs pa 'byung //ring 'grel rnam dbye mang po yang //ye shes dri med phu bas ston pa byed//de bzhin ma 'ongs dus kyi yang //mi'i seng ge mtha'

106a3(phal chen): gsum gshegs pa'i sems can gtso bo rnams//ji snyed skyes par gyur pa dang //de dag thams cad ma lus rab tu shes//de ltar rab tu shes pas na//mtha' dang dbung

106a7(phal chen): med sems rnams yang dag 'dzud pa byed//shin tu yangs pa'i tshad med las//'du shes de dag de la yang dag bsgrubs//nyon mongs med dang nyon mongs dang //slob dpon 
106b1(phal chen): mi slob sems kyang de bzhin rab tu shes//sems rgyud de dag re re la yang //sems rnams brjod du med pa mngon 'tshang rgya//gcig dang gnyis su shes pa med//de dag yongs su 
106b2(phal chen): 'dres par 'gyur ba'ang med pa ste//so so'i 'du shes rnam pa la//sems can de dag skyes par gyur pa yin//'di na 'dug par mthong gyur kyang //sems can brjod med gzhan 
106b3(phal chen): dang gzhan sems ldan pa'i rnams//gzhan gyi zhing phyin 'du shes kyang //'jig rten sna tshogs rnams la rab tu skye//'di 'dra'i tshul la 'jug pa la//spyod pa'i mchog gis
